A 30k-sample subset further provides structured reasoning traces linking key frames and changed regions to final answers. We develop LongEarth through supervised fine-tuning with explicit sequence identifiers and structured chain-of-thought supervision. Building on LongEarth, LongEarth-R1 applies group relative policy optimization with format, temporal, and spatial rewards. LongEarth-R1 achieves the best results on all 12 long-sequence tasks while remaining competitive on standard remote sensing benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [Rules or Character? Scaling Laws for AI Safety Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13345)
*Satoshi Takahashi,Nobuji Kouno,Masaaki Komatsu,Ryuji Hamamoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文构建了一个将安全资源在训练期“角色塑造”和推理期“规则执行”之间分配的理论模型。核心结论是：最优配置主要由角色塑造在新颖条件下的脆弱性决定，而非部署规模本身；规模增大通常仅小幅提高对角色塑造的最优投入比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人工智能安全实践常同时采用训练期行为对齐与推理期输出约束，但两者的资源配置缺少形式化指导。论文旨在解释部署规模、过滤器退化、共同模式失效及行为塑造脆弱性如何共同影响最优安全架构。

Method: 作者以资源分配参数α∈[0,1]表示投入角色塑造的比例，剩余资源投入规则执行；构造包含规模相关过滤器退化、共同模式失效和角色脆弱性的比较静态模型。在乘性帕累托损失设定下推导期望损失闭式表达，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟计算条件风险价值（CVaR），比较乐观、中等与悲观三类情景中的最优α。

Result: 最优α通常为内部解或完全偏向规则执行的边界解。随着部署规模T增加，最优配置会向角色塑造略微移动，幅度因情景而异，约为Δα*=+0.01至+0.21。基准角色脆弱率p^(0)_frag是最关键参数：其变化可使最优α移动0.50，显著超过尾部损失严重度、过滤器质量和共同模式失效概率的影响。大规模部署时，基于CVaR与基于期望损失得到的最优解趋于一致。

Conclusion: 安全架构设计不应仅依据部署规模来决定训练期对齐与推理期规则的配比，更应优先评估角色塑造在分布外或新颖条件下的稳定性。若角色脆弱性较高，规则执行应占更大比重；提升角色塑造的稳健性可能比单纯扩展过滤能力更能改变最优安全投入结构。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) safety systems combine character shaping (e.g., Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback [RLHF], Constitutional AI), which modifies behavioral distributions at training time, with rule enforcement (e.g., output filters, safety classifiers), which blocks harmful outputs at inference time, yet little formal analysis exists on how their optimal balance should change as deployment scales increase. We introduce a stylized comparative-statics model that parameterizes safety design as a resource allocation alpha in [0,1] between these two approaches, incorporating scale-dependent filter degradation, common-mode failures, and character fragility -- the risk that shaped behavior degrades or collapses under novel conditions. Under a multiplicative Pareto damage model, we derive closed-form expected harm and supplement it with tail-risk (CVaR) analysis via Monte Carlo simulation. Across three scenarios (optimistic, moderate, pessimistic), the optimal alpha* is interior or at the rules-only boundary and shifts weakly toward character shaping as deployment scale T grows, from negligible (Delta alpha* = +0.01) to pronounced (Delta alpha* = +0.21) depending on scenario. The dominant parameter is the baseline character fragility rate p^(0)_frag, which shifts alpha* by 0.50 across its range -- far exceeding the effect of tail severity, filter quality, or common-mode failure probability. CVaR and expected-harm optima converge at large T. These results suggest that safety architecture decisions depend less on deployment scale per se than on the reliability of character shaping under distributional shift.

</details>


### [98] [Jointly Predicting Courses and Grades Using a Transformer-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13409)
*Paul Savala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TRACE（学业课程成绩估计Transformer），将学生某学期并行修读的课程建模为集合，并联合预测下一学期的选课集合与对应成绩。相较仅预测成绩的同构模型，联合任务使平均绝对误差降低近50%，且优于LSTM序列模型和图神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有学习分析模型常将学业记录简化为线性课程序列，忽略同一学期课程之间的并发关系及课程负荷影响。这会削弱对修读高负荷或高难度课程学生的成绩预测准确性，因此需要能够按学期刻画课程组合并同时推断选课与成绩的模型。

Method: TRACE采用Transformer架构，以学期为粒度编码课程历史，从而表示同一学期内课程的共现与并发效应。模型执行多任务联合学习：一项任务预测下一学期的课程集合，另一项任务预测这些课程的成绩；训练时使用结合课程集合预测损失与成绩预测损失的新型联合损失函数。模型还可自然融合学生属性特征，并通过重新训练与校准适配新机构数据。

Result: 在十年机构历史数据上，TRACE相对于仅进行成绩预测的相同架构，将平均绝对误差降低近50%。实验还显示，其整体表现优于传统LSTM类序列预测方法及基于图神经网络的方法。结果表明，训练阶段显式预测学生将修读的课程，是提升成绩预测质量的关键因素。

Conclusion: 课程并发性是学业表现预测中不可忽略的结构信息。以学期课程集合建模，并将选课预测与成绩预测联合优化，能够显著提高预测精度。TRACE为构建可解释、可迁移的高校早期识别与学业支持系统提供了有效的技术路径，但跨机构部署仍需基于当地数据进行重训练和校准。

Abstract: Existing predictive models in learning analytics often treat student academic history as a simple sequence, overlooking the concurrent nature of courses taken within a semester. This simplification can lead to inaccurate performance predictions, particularly for students with heavy or challenging course loads. This paper introduces a TRansformer for Academic Course-grade Estimation (TRACE) that addresses this limitation by jointly predicting both the set of courses a student will take and their corresponding grades for an upcoming semester. Our approach encodes courses on a per-semester basis to capture the effects of course concurrency and utilizes a novel loss function combining course-set prediction with grade prediction. We demonstrate that predicting courses taken in addition to the grades in those courses leads to significant improvements in prediction quality. Trained on ten years of institutional data, our joint prediction model reduces mean absolute error by nearly 50% compared to an identical architecture that predicts grades alone. The model also outperforms traditional LSTM-based sequential models, as well as graph neural network-based approaches, and offers natural ways to incorporate student attribute data. This work demonstrates the utility of modern neural architectures for creating interpretable models that can be adapted to new institutions via retraining and recalibration, as well as the importance of key techniques, such as predicting courses taken during training. We discuss how this model could be incorporated into early detection systems at institutions of higher education.

</details>


### [99] [Who Speaks Matters: Authority-Aware Multi-View RAG over Italian Parliamentary Proceedings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13410)
*Mirko Tritella,Riccardo Pozzi,Matteo Palmonari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Parliamentary proceedings are a primary record of democratic deliberation, yet their volume and fragmentation make multi-perspective access difficult for citizens, journalists, and researchers. Applying Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to parliamentary transcripts introduces three specific risks: dominance of the most frequent speakers, inability to weight speakers according to topical expertise, and citation misattribution in politically sensitive text. We present ParliamentRAG, a RAG system for the Italian Chamber of Deputies that addresses these risks jointly. Its core contribution is a topic-dependent authority model that estimates each speaker's authority as a function of the current query, combining interpretable components such as profession, education, and previous interventions. Given a user query, the system retrieves relevant speech chunks, identifies topic-relevant experts across parliamentary groups, and generates a summary synthesizing their perspectives, accompanied by supporting quotations. ParliamentRAG is evaluated against Google NotebookLM on 15 policy topics via a two-level protocol combining automated metrics and blind A/B human evaluation by six domain experts. The system achieves higher coverage across political groups (0.97 vs. 0.95), perfect quotation faithfulness (1.00 vs. 0.95), and stronger expert preferences on source-related dimensions, while NotebookLM remains stronger on prose-oriented dimensions.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Final Scores: A Systematic Evaluation of Agents for Long-Horizon AI Research and Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13417)
*Yiwei Li,Wanli Yang,Hexiang Tan,Xiangzhou Huang,Zhengyu Chen,Ziran Li,Borun Chen,Shanglin Lei,Huaisheng Zhu,Hao Tian,Fei Sun,Xunliang Cai,Jingang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套针对长程技术研发型智能体的系统评测框架，在36项任务上比较7个前沿模型。结论是：当前智能体更接近能够执行工程优化的系统，而非具备稳定自主研究能力的系统；其方案实现能力较强，但稳定性、方法创新性与经验复用可靠性仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅以最终得分评估智能体，无法揭示能力提升或失败发生于何种过程环节，也无法判断历史经验是否真正改善后续决策。因此，作者希望从过程行为与经验复用两个维度，更完整地刻画长程实验智能体的实际能力边界。

Method: 作者构建了覆盖36个长程任务的评测，并对7个模型进行比较。框架采用基于规则的过程指标，将单次运行拆分为“方案构建、执行、反馈控制”三个阶段进行分析；同时通过受控对比实验，评估智能体在同一任务内及跨任务场景中复用既有经验的效果。

Result: 模型通常能够提出并实现可行的工程方案，但不同运行之间的表现波动显著。表现最好的方案主要是对已有技术进行适配、调整或组合，较少产生真正的方法学创新。即使最终结果相近，不同模型或运行过程也可能受制于不同瓶颈。经验复用有时能提高后续决策质量，但也可能引入误导；此外，评测与执行支架的设计会明显影响性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 长程智能体的评估应从终局分数扩展到过程质量、稳定性与经验迁移能力。提升方向包括：针对过程瓶颈改进训练目标，设计更有效的推理时策略，建立可靠的经验存储与检索机制，以及优化执行支架以降低运行方差并增强反馈控制能力。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly capable of improving models, systems, and other technical artifacts through long-horizon experimentation. To understand the current state of this capability, however, evaluation must go beyond final scores, which neither reveal where progress is gained or lost nor indicate whether accumulated experience improves later decisions. We therefore present a systematic evaluation of seven frontier models on 36 long-horizon tasks based on a new framework that uses rule-based metrics to characterize within-run behavior through Solution Framing, Execution, and Feedback Control and controlled comparisons to assess experience reuse within and across tasks. The results show that current agents operate more like engineering optimizers than fully autonomous researchers: they can formulate and implement practical solutions, but their performance varies substantially across runs, their strongest solutions mainly adapt or combine established techniques, and genuine methodological novelty remains rare. Detailed analysis reveals that observed performance is shaped by multiple factors, including distinct process bottlenecks behind similar final outcomes, experience reuse that can help or mislead subsequent decisions, and harness designs that affect performance stability. These findings suggest concrete directions for improving model training, inference-time strategies, experience management, and harness design.

</details>


### [101] [Enhancing Virtual Agents through SLMs and Edge-Computing: An Exploratory Evaluation of Think and Memory Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13420)
*Aimilios Hadjiliasi,Louis Nisiotis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了一种面向边缘设备的具身智能虚拟代理网关：以认知具身代理架构（CEAA）为蓝图，利用不同规模的Qwen2.5小型语言模型支持“思考”和“记忆”模块，从而在沉浸式虚拟环境中实现具有一定上下文感知与持续交互能力的代理原型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂虚拟世界中的长期运行代理需要感知、记忆、推理、规划与行动等认知能力，但将这些能力部署为低延迟、可持续且具备上下文适应性的系统存在工程挑战。云端大模型通常带来时延、成本与部署依赖问题，因此论文考察能否以边缘侧小型语言模型承担关键认知编排任务。

Method: 作者依据CEAA选择“Think”和“Memory”两个核心过程，开发边缘侧虚拟代理网关，并部署于NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX。系统采用不同参数规模的Qwen2.5模型处理服务请求、路由决策和基于记忆的多轮对话；通过仿真实验测量请求路由准确率、记忆读取表现及端到端延迟，以比较模型规模与边缘执行效率之间的关系。

Result: 实验表明，该原型能够在边缘设备上完成服务请求处理和记忆驱动对话，并对路由准确率、记忆读取能力和响应时延进行了量化评估。结果支持如下可行性：SLM可以在资源受限的边缘环境中承担CEAA部分认知流程，为虚拟代理提供初步的上下文关联与持续性能力。摘要未给出具体数值，因而无法判断各模型配置间的精确性能差异。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了以SLM结合边缘计算实现CEAA局部模块的工程路径，为沉浸式虚拟世界中的具身代理提供了可部署的“认知大脑”原型。其贡献主要是架构落地与边缘性能验证；由于仅覆盖“思考”和“记忆”流程，尚未完整实现感知、规划和具身行动闭环，后续需评估更复杂环境下的长期记忆可靠性、任务泛化能力及多模块协同。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent virtual agents are expected to operate as persistent, adaptive, and context-aware entities within complex virtual and Metaverse worlds. However, implementing cognitively capable agents in such environments is conceptually and technologically challenging. Among a range of blueprints and development approaches, the Cognitive Embodied Agent Architecture (CEAA) has been developed as an implementation-oriented framework for architecting components of perception, memory, reasoning, planning, and embodied action. Considering the recent advances in edge computing and generative AI language models, this paper explores the use of Small Language Models (SLMs) to support edge-based operation of selected CEAA components, focusing on "Think" and "Memory" as processes central to cognitive orchestration and persistence of virtual agents in interactive virtual worlds. An edge-based virtual agent gateway system was developed and evaluated on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX using Qwen2.5 models of different sizes, exploring the system's capability to process service requests and handle memory-driven conversations. A series of simulation experiments evaluated routing accuracy, memory-read performance, and latency, demonstrating an SLM-driven prototype agent system that partially implements selected CEAA processes to support the development of embodied agents whose cognitive "brain" can operate efficiently and contextually for interactive experiences in immersive virtual worlds.

</details>


### [102] [RAIL: An Automatic Classifier of the Artificial Intelligence Readiness Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13428)
*Juan Irving Vasquez,Juan Terven,Laura-Ivoone Garay-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出统一人工智能就绪度等级（AIRL）与基于独立大语言模型专家的评估方法（RAIL），旨在将自然语言描述转化为可复核、可自动化的九级AI技术成熟度判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI成熟度框架存在三类不足：传统技术就绪度等级缺少AI专属门槛；机器学习技术就绪度等级依赖内部流程材料；AI与数据就绪度维度模型的量表难以横向比较。因此，需要一种无需获取内部工件、可由文本描述直接判定且具备统一标尺的方法。

Method: AIRL以九级有序量表整合三类既有框架，核心依据为环境证据阶梯，并设置规格定义、数据存在性、数据质量、数据合法性、领域专家知识和算法成熟度六项维度上限；同时通过通用性锚定规则与明确的赋级纪律约束判定。RAIL采用专家面板结构：一个证据代理与六个维度代理分别独立评估，再由确定性的最小值规则聚合；首席专家仅可确认或下调结果，不得突破维度上限。

Result: 在若干研究工作的测试中，该方法表现出较一致的等级判定，并能减少单体大语言模型分类器可能产生的成熟度高估。摘要未提供具体样本规模、标注基准、准确率、一致性系数或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: AIRL为AI项目成熟度提供了面向文本、可解释且受多维约束的统一评估框架；RAIL通过独立专家分工、保守聚合和不可上调的复核机制提升了评估稳健性。其有效性仍需借助更大规模、多领域数据集及人工专家基准进一步验证。

Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [103] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [104] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出“因果世界模型”（Causal World Models, CWMs）框架，主张智能体的世界模型不应仅具备生成或预测能力，还应表征实体、实体间关系及实体与环境的交互机制，以支持因果推理、规划与分布外决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型常以观测预测或生成质量为主要目标，但这类模型未必揭示环境动力学背后的稳定结构，因此难以可靠地解释变化、应对干预或迁移至训练分布之外。论文旨在用因果视角统一世界建模，并明确何种表征能够服务于决策任务。

Method: 作者从多个抽象层次考察世界模型：由感知观测出发，逐步到实体级表征、交互关系及环境动力学的概念结构。论文以目标任务为基础形式化定义因果世界模型，并将其与因果表征学习、以对象为中心的学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策方法建立联系；同时借助可识别性理论，讨论模型各组成部分能否从数据中恢复及其可恢复的等价类范围。

Result: 论文的主要产出是概念性与形式化框架，而非特定实验系统或量化性能结果。其核心结论是：有效世界模型应显式或隐式捕获决定系统演化的因果结构，包括实体属性、实体间交互和实体—环境交互；仅优化生成能力不足以保证模型具备因果推理和可靠决策能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为世界模型提供了以任务支持能力为中心的因果定义，并指出可识别性是判断因果结构能否从数据中学习到的关键条件。该框架有助于将世界建模从观测层面的拟合推进到可解释、可干预、可迁移的结构化表示，从而支持更稳健的规划与决策。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [105] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个面向临床推理的开源多智能体编排框架，以确定性的分阶段协作替代单体式大模型提示，并通过可追溯的中间结果提升可解释性、可配置性与问题定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体提示方式难以清晰分离信息抽取、推理、回答生成和评估等环节，发生错误时不易归因；同时，临床领域使用者往往缺乏编程与提示工程经验，因而需要低代码、模型无关且可部署于不同计算环境的解决方案。

Method: 框架将任务拆分为角色专门化智能体，采用显式上下文传递和确定性编排执行抽取、推理、答案生成及评估。其Decomposer模块根据自然语言任务描述自动生成面向任务的智能体提示，减少人工提示设计。系统通过YAML完成配置，无需修改代码，并兼容API模型及本地CPU部署。

Result: 摘要声称MARC可提供阶段级失败归因和可追溯中间输出，支持模型无关的多智能体临床推理流程，并降低临床专家使用门槛。论文摘要未报告具体数据集、比较基线、定量指标、实验结果或成本与延迟分析，因此实际性能增益尚无法据此评估。

Conclusion: MARC的核心贡献是将临床推理工作流工程化为可解释、可审计和易配置的多智能体系统，并通过自动提示生成改善可用性。其价值主要体现在流程透明性与部署灵活性；后续应通过严格的临床任务评测验证准确性、鲁棒性、可重复性及实际应用效益。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [106] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该报告在不改变主干架构、分块自回归生成机制和训练数据的前提下，重构了条件控制模块。核心思想是让条件信号在潜表示和时间结构上尽量与目标视频一致，从而提升条件融合的一致性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旧版条件信号与生成视频之间可能存在表征域和时序统计不匹配：静态图像条件难以传递运动信息，基于深度变形的空间记忆表达受限，训练与推理阶段的VAE处理协议也可能造成分布偏差。因此，需要将视觉条件、空间记忆和生成目标统一到相同的因果VAE潜空间及连续时序框架中。

Method: 方法包括两项核心替换：以流式3D点缓存渲染器取代深度变形空间记忆；以与生成视频相同的因果VAE潜空间编码视觉条件。具体实施为：将静态帧条件改为运动感知潜变量条件；将重渲染空间记忆按连续序列进行因果编码；在像素空间对齐时间记忆窗口；采用删除记忆令牌的硬记忆丢弃而非将其置零；统一训练和推理时的VAE编码、解码协议；移除相机AdaLN分支，并仅通过重渲染的空间条件实现视角控制。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果或具体指标，但其设计预期可减少条件信息与视频潜变量之间的表征和时序错配，增强运动、空间记忆及视角控制的连贯性，并降低训练—推理不一致带来的性能损失。

Conclusion: 该版本的主要贡献不是扩大模型或数据规模，而是通过条件表示统一化和时序一致化改造条件管线。将空间条件、视觉条件与视频生成统一到因果VAE潜空间，并以流式3D点缓存承担空间记忆，有望形成更稳定、更一致且更易控的视频生成系统。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [107] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出OmniScientist：一种可从异构原始科学证据直接开展研究的端到端全模态AI科学家系统。其在36个真实数据案例中完成从原始数据到论文编译的完整流程；相较仅接收预计算标量特征的对照版本，直接感知原始证据在全部7项评价维度上提升，并在85%的成对比较中获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科研系统虽已覆盖假设生成、代码执行和论文撰写等环节，但主要依赖文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要，难以获取对科学判断关键的空间、时间、跨通道及实验过程关系。因此，研究旨在使智能体能够以原始、多模态证据为基础，贯穿科研全生命周期地形成可追溯、可验证的结论。

Method: 系统由感知层及构思、实验、写作三个自治智能体构成，并置于确定性流水线中。感知层处理图像、信号、音频、视频、三维结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等原始输入；三个智能体分别负责提出问题与假设、设计和执行分析实验、生成论文。系统还通过代码化检查实施创意筛查、统计严谨性验证、执行来源记录和数值可追溯性控制。评估覆盖5类学科、4类科学证据家族的36个真实数据案例，并与只使用预计算标量特征的消融版本进行配对比较。

Result: OmniScientist在36个案例中均完成了由原始数据到可编译论文的全流程，使用参考推理骨干时平均论文综合评分为6.3。与标量特征对照版本相比，直接原始证据感知在7个评价维度上均有改进，并在成对主观判断中取得85%的胜率。结果表明，原始多模态证据并非仅提升输入信息量，还能改善研究问题设定、实验决策及最终论断的证据支撑。

Conclusion: 该工作主张，全生命周期的多模态感知是实现证据驱动型AI科研的重要条件。OmniScientist展示了将原始异构数据、可执行严谨性检查和科研工作流整合的可行路径；但摘要未说明各学科上的细粒度性能差异、评分量表与人工评审协议，以及在更复杂开放环境中的稳健性，仍需通过更透明的基准和复现实验进一步验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SynWeaver是一种面向网页智能体的数据合成框架，通过构建网站地图、注入网站先验并协同生成任务与轨迹，提升合成监督数据的覆盖性、真实性和可执行性，从而改善未见网站上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于探索的数据合成方法虽能减少人工标注，但通常无法充分覆盖网站功能；同时因缺少网站特定知识，易提出与页面实际能力不符的任务，导致下游轨迹数据的多样性、效率与语义可靠性受限。

Method: 框架首先对目标网站进行结构化探索，构建覆盖多类功能页面状态及可执行交互的网站地图；随后从地图中提取页面级与状态转移级监督，训练具备界面感知能力且融入网站先验的模型，以生成更具依据的任务；最后联合合成任务和执行轨迹，在二者不一致时进行协同更新，并通过验证与修复机制筛选出可执行且语义对齐的监督样本。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager上的实验表明，SynWeaver相较于强数据合成基线具有稳定优势，并能生成对站内与跨站泛化均更有效的训练监督。摘要未提供具体性能数值、消融实验细节或计算成本。

Conclusion: 网站地图及其派生监督能够为任务生成提供关键的网站先验；将任务描述与交互轨迹进行闭环协同校正，可缓解任务虚构和轨迹失配问题。该工作表明，高覆盖率、可验证的数据合成是提升网页智能体泛化能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [109] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文以一个超大规模生产级TypeScript应用的单案例，报告AI编程代理在“先规格、后实现、再审计”的流程下完成核心架构重构。作者声称该变更突破了传统渐进式重构的可行边界，但证据主要来自过程日志、静态审计和有限运行观察，尚不足以证明普遍有效性或长期正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究针对一个难题：当核心生命周期不变量深度耦合于大量模块时，传统局部修改往往风险极高，通常被认为需要重写。目标是验证AI代理能否通过可冻结、可审计的形式化规格，将生成任务与人工代码审查解耦，并在大型真实代码库中完成跨模块行为迁移。

Method: 系统规模为717,725行、3,648个文件。任务是解除“AI请求期间UI面板必须保持打开”的生命周期约束，使流式生成在面板关闭后继续运行，并可在重新打开时重新附着至同一实时流而不丢失或重复。流程包括：代理生成规格；14轮规格—源码一致性审计；一次原子化实现；编译与测试反馈；17轮实现—冻结规格验证。31轮审计中修复201项缺陷，以连续两轮零发现作为经验收敛标准。变更涉及189个文件，其中31个为新增文件；计入抽取阶段的两次提交共涉及288个文件、34,770行新增和16,422行删除。

Result: 作者报告代理在三天内、约2,430美元成本下完成变更；在首次及后续约30次会话中，软件均表现符合目标行为，未观察到缺陷。论文还公开了超过1,500页的法语规格与原始会话记录，以支持过程复核及借助语言模型进行一致性检查。结果表明，规格驱动的多轮审计可能显著提升AI代理处理大范围架构改造时的可控性与可追溯性。

Conclusion: 该案例的核心贡献是展示一种以规格冻结、反复审计和缺陷收敛为中心的AI辅助重构工作流，而非单纯依赖一次性代码生成。但结论应谨慎解读：这是单一系统、单一任务和作者自评成功的证据；“无预先行为预言机”意味着验证仍高度依赖规格本身的完整性；连续两轮零发现不能等同于不存在遗漏；约30次会话的运行观察也不能替代系统化回归、边界条件、并发与长期稳定性评估。后续研究应在多项目、多任务、独立评审和可量化行为测试下，与人工重构及其他代理流程进行对照。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [110] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文首次系统研究由多模态大语言模型生成网页的跨浏览器、跨设备渲染兼容性问题，构建WebCompat数据集并提出离线检测器XCompat。结果显示，68%的生成网页存在至少一种兼容性缺陷，XCompat在测试集上取得0.903的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页生成评测主要在固定浏览器和设备环境下衡量视觉相似度，无法反映真实部署中不同浏览器、屏幕尺寸与设备条件下的渲染可靠性。论文旨在填补AI生成前端页面跨环境兼容性评测与自动检测的空白。

Method: 作者构建WebCompat数据集，包含2,032个标注实例：由8种代表性AI工具生成的网页，在9种浏览器—设备组合中渲染。研究从兼容性问题发生比例、用户可感知症状及代码层根因三个角度进行分析。在此基础上，提出轻量级离线检测器XCompat，联合使用网页截图的视觉信息与DOM树的结构信息判断兼容性缺陷。

Result: 68%的AI生成网页至少存在一种跨环境兼容性问题。最常见的是破坏整体页面布局的缺陷，占88.3%，主要表现为页面被直接缩放以适应屏幕、字体过小，或缩放比例不匹配导致内容被截断。局部元素问题，如图像变形或组件缺失，占13.4%，相对较少。尽管多数模型会生成响应式设计相关代码模式，但其实现往往不正确。XCompat在WebCompat-test上获得0.903的F1分数，优于现有兼容性检查工具及大语言模型基线。

Conclusion: 仅以固定环境中的视觉保真度评估AI网页生成能力是不充分的，跨环境渲染兼容性应成为前端代码生成系统的重要可靠性指标。WebCompat为该问题提供了标准化数据与分析依据，XCompat则展示了结合视觉截图和DOM结构进行自动化检测的有效性；后续工作应着重提升模型对响应式布局与跨浏览器渲染规则的正确实现能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted to automate webpage generation from visual designs (e.g., screenshots). However, existing evaluations are limited to visual fidelity assessment under a fixed browser-device configuration. Such a setting overlooks the cross-environment rendering compatibility for real-world deployments.
  To address this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study of cross-environment compatibility in AI-generated webpages. Specifically, we construct WebCompat, a dataset of 2,032 annotated instances, comprising webpages generated by 8 representative AI tools, each rendered across 9 browser-and-device combinations. We analyze the prevalence of compatibility issues, their user-perceptible symptoms, and underlying code-level root causes. Our findings reveal that 68% of generated webpages exhibit at least one compatibility issue, underscoring the pervasive reliability concerns surrounding MLLM-generated front-end artifacts. The most prevalent symptoms are failures that disrupt the entire page layout (88.3%): pages shrink directly to fit the target screen with too small fonts, or exhibit scale mismatches that produce cut-off content. Failures localized to individual elements, such as image distortion or missing components, are comparatively less common (13.4%). Furthermore, although most MLLMs incorporate responsive design patterns into the generation, they fail to properly implement these codes.
  Guided by the findings, we develop XCompat, a lightweight offline compatibility issue detector that combines visual screenshots and the structural DOM tree for analysis. It achieves an F1 score of 0.903 on the WebCompat-test, outperforming the existing compatibility checking tools and LLM baselines. All datasets and tools are released to support future research on rendering reliability in MLLM-based front-end code generation.

</details>


### [111] [Memorization Diagnostics for Code LLMs Should be Scale-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12771)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Abdoul Aziz Bonkoungou,Alberick Euraste Djiré,Xunzhu Tang,Yewei Song,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,El Hacen Diallo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文指出，现有用于检测代码大模型记忆化的探测方法在模型规模增大后普遍失效；作者认为其根因是将“表征负载”与“记忆化”混为一谈，并主张未来评测应将二者显式分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码大模型是否依赖训练样本记忆而非真实理解仍有争议，但同义词扰动、代码插入冗余片段及基于对数概率的探测，在已知受训练数据影响的基准上也难以影响大模型。论文试图解释这种随规模出现的探测失灵现象，并重新界定记忆化评估的实际意义。

Method: 作者使用数值问题，并施加可逆数学变换以改变输入的表面表示形式。在该设置下，表征负载可由变换带来的表示适应需求体现，而记忆化可通过答案或解法族的一致性加以区分；随后比较不同规模编码器在此类变化下的表现，分析其是否仍收敛到正确解法族。

Result: 结果表明，规模更大的编码器能够吸收较高的表征负载，并在表面形式变化后仍得到正确的解法族。因此，传统扰动探测对小模型有效、对大模型无效，未必意味着大模型没有记忆化，更可能是其表示鲁棒性抵消了扰动影响。记忆化会抬高受训练数据影响的基准分数，但单纯依赖扰动或对数概率的检测难以可靠量化这种影响。

Conclusion: 论文认为，实际软件工程更关心模型面对不同表面表达时能否产出可用、可泛化的功能性答案，而非答案是否曾在训练中出现。未来评估应分别测量表征负载适应能力与记忆化，而不能使用会隐式耦合二者的传统探针；摘要的核心贡献是提出这一评测框架转向，而非证明记忆化本身不重要。

Abstract: The extent to which large language models for code rely on memorization over genuine understanding remains highly debated. While current literature frequently reports widespread memorization, evaluating the underlying probing techniques across dense architectures reveals a severe breakdown in their utility at scale. Traditional encoder-style probes using perturbations such as synonym fuzzing or dead-code insertion struggle to expose memorization in scaled models, even on known-contaminated benchmarks, and decoder-style probes that rely on log probabilities show similar performance degradation. The specific mode of failure for these probes, particularly why such techniques disrupt smaller models but fail to impact larger ones, motivates us to untangle representation load from memorization rather than treating them as a single phenomenon. By applying invertible mathematical transforms to numeric problems, we isolate these two factors and reveal that scaled encoders successfully absorb substantial representation load while still converging on the correct family of solutions. In practical software engineering, this ability to adapt to varying surface forms is what truly matters for usability and generalizability in LLM and agentic applications. Whether a specific solution was seen during training becomes a much less pressing question because although memorization inflates scores on contaminated benchmarks, factoring out representation load makes it debatable how much we should truly care if a functional answer was originally memorized. Future evaluations must therefore be built around separating these phenomena rather than relying on methodologies that quietly entangle them.

</details>


### [112] [Dissecting Software Graphs: Structural Insights for Driver-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12859)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Haipeng Cai,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文研究多执行模式程序的多驱动模糊测试，提出以静态调用图为共享骨架、将各驱动动态覆盖投影为驱动诱导子图的结构分析框架。对27个C/C++项目、43个可执行文件和854种驱动配置的实验表明，在相同总预算下，多驱动策略显著优于最佳单驱动策略，但不同驱动的结构贡献高度不均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模糊测试评估主要采用代码覆盖率和缺陷数量，难以解释不同命令行选项、子命令或配置模式如何划分、重叠或遗漏程序结构，也难以据此指导驱动选择与资源分配。论文旨在将多驱动测试从单纯的覆盖累积问题转化为可量化的软件结构探索问题。

Method: 方法分为四阶段：构建静态调用图骨架；对各驱动进行模糊测试与动态覆盖剖析；将驱动覆盖映射至调用图，形成驱动诱导子图并计算凝聚性、碎片化、模块度、重叠度和剩余未探索区域等图指标；围绕研究问题比较单驱动与多驱动策略。实验采用来自OSS-Fuzz的27个C/C++项目，覆盖43个目标程序与854种驱动配置。

Result: 在相同总测试预算下，多驱动模糊测试相较最佳单驱动基线，使被覆盖的调用图节点数提高27.9%，控制流图边覆盖率提高73.5%，并发现11个单驱动大多未触及的独有缺陷或异常行为。分析同时显示，驱动间的贡献差异显著；其诱导子图在凝聚性、碎片化、模块度和重叠程度上存在较大变化；未充分探索的结构并非均质长尾，而是呈现可重复识别的若干模式。

Conclusion: 多驱动模糊测试的核心不只是增加测试入口或合并覆盖率，而是有效探索由不同驱动共同覆盖的软件结构。静态调用图与动态覆盖结合的诱导子图抽象能够揭示驱动互补性、冗余性及盲区，为后续基于结构特征进行驱动筛选、预算调度和测试策略优化提供依据。

Abstract: Many software systems expose multiple execution modes through command-line options, subcommands, and configuration flags. For such programs, fuzzing depends on both mutated inputs and the invoked mode. Yet evaluations still focus on coverage and bug counts, leaving unclear how execution modes partition, overlap, and miss software structure, and how these differences affect effectiveness. We present an empirical study of software structure under multi-driver fuzzing. We propose a structural abstraction that uses a static call graph as a shared backbone and projects driver-specific dynamic coverage onto it to derive driver-induced subgraphs. Based on this abstraction, we develop a four-phase methodology for backbone construction, fuzzing and profiling, graph-based analysis, and research-question-driven evaluation. We apply it to 27 OSS-Fuzz-derived C/C++ projects, spanning 43 executables and 854 driver configurations. Under the same total budget, multi-driver fuzzing outperforms the best single-driver baseline, increasing covered call-graph nodes by 27.9% and CFG-edge coverage by 73.5%, and revealing 11 unique bugs and abnormal behaviors largely missed by single-driver fuzzing. However, driver contributions are uneven, subgraphs differ substantially in cohesion, fragmentation, modularity, overlap, and residual under-exploration follows recurring regimes rather than a homogeneous tail. These results show that multi-driver fuzzing is fundamentally a structural exploration problem.

</details>


### [113] [Requirements-Augmented Generation for Trustworthy Acceptance Testing of LLM-Based Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12970)
*Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Zhenping Xie,Yonghui Liu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Aldeida Aleti,Siwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大语言模型软件（LBS）的自动化验收测试框架：通过需求增强生成构造上下文感知测试预言机，并以置信度校准的级联判决控制结论可靠性。工业案例显示，该方法在预言机质量、判决准确率与成本效率上均具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LBS的输出具有随机性和强上下文依赖性；相同查询在不同用户画像、软件状态和领域约束下可能需要不同答案。因此，传统依赖确定预期输出的验收测试及测试预言机难以适用。核心需求是自动理解用户指令和意图，并在需求或环境变化时对测试结论的可信度进行量化控制。

Method: 方法包含两项技术。其一，Requirements-Augmented Generation（REAG）：使用自适应检索增强生成，从软件需求、领域知识和用户画像中检索相关信息，并结合自推理生成上下文感知的测试预言机。其二，置信度校准级联判决：通过模拟专家间一致性估计判决可靠性；高置信度结论直接接受，模糊样本升级处理，低置信度样本拒绝判定。该可靠性控制由保形风险控制提供经验性保证。

Result: 在生产级营养咨询应用的案例中，REAG获得3.91/5的预言机质量评分，82%的样本达到合格或边际合格质量。级联判决达到98.8%的准确率；通过筛除不合格预言机，将质量评分由3.91提升至4.30；相较单一判别器基线，成本效率提升31.7%。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，将需求检索、上下文推理与置信度校准结合，可为LBS构建更适应用户意图和软件语境的自动化验收测试机制。其主要贡献不只是生成测试预言机，还在于以可控风险处理不确定结论，从而提升工业部署中的可靠性与成本效益。

Abstract: LLM-based software (LBS) integrates large language models as core components to deliver flexible, personalised responses. Unlike traditional software with deterministic outputs, LBSs exhibit context-dependent, stochastic behaviour that renders classical acceptance testing and test oracles insufficient: the same query may require fundamentally different responses depending on user personas and software context. This gap creates an urgent need for automated acceptance testing frameworks that autonomously interpret user instructions, while reliably inferring user intentions in a changing environment. In this paper, we present an automated acceptance testing framework for LBS with calibrated verdict reliability via two technical contributions. First, we introduce Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG), which interprets user intentions by retrieving relevant software requirements, domain knowledge, and personas via adaptive RAG and self-reasoning to generate context-aware test oracles. Second, recognising that oracle generation may retrieve irrelevant constraints, misinterpret intent, or hallucinate requirements, we introduce a confidence-calibrated cascade judgment. This method quantifies verdict reliability via simulated expert agreement -- accepting high-confidence verdicts, escalating ambiguous cases, or abstaining when uncertain -- with empirical reliability guarantees backed by conformal risk control. An industrial case study on a production nutrition advisory application demonstrates that REAG achieves a 3.91/5 oracle quality score, reaching qualified or marginal oracle quality in 82% of cases. The confidence-calibrated cascade achieves 98.8% accuracy, improves oracle quality from 3.91 to 4.30 by filtering unqualified outputs, and delivers a 31.7% cost-efficiency improvement over single-judge baselines, validating industrial viability

</details>


### [114] [How Powerful are LLMs in Generating Formal Program Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13077)
*Fanpeng Yang,Xing Li,Shuling Wang,Jie An,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng,Wenhan Wang,Weiyi Wang,Naijun Zhan,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Coins，一个基于Rocq的规格说明评测框架，通过在可信测试用例上实例化待评规格并生成具体证明义务，评估大语言模型生成程序形式化规格的质量。研究指出，规格生成仍很困难，且传统验证中的证明复杂度会掩盖规格质量差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证依赖精确规格，但人工编写成本高。现有对模型生成规格的评测通常要求验证实现满足规格，或证明两个规格语义等价；这些任务本身证明难度极高，容易将“证明是否困难”误判为“规格是否优劣”。因此，需要一种能更直接、稳定地区分规格质量的评测方法。

Method: Coins以人工整理的Rocq规格和可信测试用例为基础。对于模型生成的候选规格，框架将其在测试输入上具体化，并自动构造相应的证明义务；成功完成证明可作为候选规格在该测试情形下正确的可靠证据。论文在HumanEval基准上开展大规模实验，并比较不同规格及验证条件下的表现，以分析规格质量与证明复杂度之间的关系。

Result: 实验显示，大语言模型的程序规格生成能力仍存在显著不足。更重要的是，验证任务的复杂度会造成评测混淆：质量不同的规格可能因证明困难而呈现相近的失败结果，因而传统端到端验证指标未必能准确反映真实规格质量。Coins的测试实例化机制能提供更具区分性的评估信号。

Conclusion: 评估规格生成能力的关键不只是增大模型规模，而是建立能够将规格质量与证明难度有效分离的评价机制。Coins利用测试用例驱动的形式化推理，契合“证明成功是强证据、证明失败含义不明确”的非对称特性，为规格合成进展提供了更忠实且更具判别力的测量方式。

Abstract: Formal verification provides strong guarantees of software correctness, but its adoption is limited by the high cost of writing precise formal specifications. While recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in theorem proving and verified code generation, their true ability to generate program specifications remains unclear. Existing evaluations require either verifying implementation conformance or proving semantic equivalence between specifications, both of which are formidably difficult and may conflate proof difficulty with specification quality. To address this problem, we introduce Coins, a Rocq based evaluation framework that assesses specification quality by instantiating specifications under evaluation on trusted test cases and generating concrete proof obligations. This design aligns with the asymmetric nature of formal reasoning, where successful proofs provide reliable evidence while proof failures are inherently ambiguous. Using Coins, we conduct a large scale study on HumanEval with a curated set of human written Rocq specifications. Our results show that specification generation remains a formidable challenge, and that verification complexity can obscure genuine differences in specification quality. Overall, we find that accurate specification evaluation, rather than model scaling alone, is central to understanding the power of LLMs for specification synthesis, and that test case based formal reasoning offers a more faithful and discriminative measure of progress.

</details>


### [115] [CAPRI: Contract-Aware Proof Repair for Isabelle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13459)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出CAPRI：面向Isabelle证明修复的契约感知工作流。其核心贡献是在Isabelle验证证明可接受性之外，引入独立检查器验证模型编辑是否遵守开发者授权的机器可读契约，并保留完整审计证据。实验显示，限制为仅编辑证明体可消除契约违规，同时保持接近完整理论编辑的修复成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Isabelle构建成功只能说明提交的理论被接受，不能保证大语言模型仅修改了被授权的内容。对于形式化验证代码，模型可能通过改动受保护文本、前提或周边定义来获得表面上的通过，因此需要将“证明正确”与“编辑合规”分离验证，并建立可追溯的修复过程。

Method: CAPRI将证明检查与编辑契约检查结合：Isabelle负责确认候选理论可构建，独立检查器负责比对候选仓库与授权编辑范围；同时保存提示词、模型提案、候选仓库、诊断信息、判定结果及哈希以支持审计。评估覆盖4个开发中的12个失败证明、5种工作流、每个任务和条件3次重复，共180次运行；另进行了事后OpenRouter实验，并以单侧精确McNemar检验比较部分配置。

Result: 180次运行中获得138个有效修复。144个通过Isabelle的终态候选中，有6个修改了受保护文本，且均来自可编辑完整理论的迭代式工作流。仅证明体接口得到29/36个有效修复且零契约违规；对应的完整理论工作流为31/36。单轮修复为22/36，后续预先冻结的迭代工作流为32/36，但这些数值比较的是完整工作流而非单一机制。事后OpenRouter实验未改善指定的Luna比较。采用匹配示例的Sol配置为33/36，高于冻结OpenAI Responses条件的29/36，但单侧精确McNemar检验未达到统计显著性（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: CAPRI表明，LLM辅助Isabelle证明修复应同时满足证明接受性和编辑授权合规性。将模型操作面限制为证明体是有效的安全—效能折中：显著避免未授权修改，并仅带来较小的修复率差距。不同模型或配置间的表面性能差异仍需更大样本验证，当前证据不足以支持显著优越性结论。

Abstract: We address the use of large language models (LLMs) to help discover Isabelle proofs. An Isabelle build establishes that the submitted theory is accepted, but not that an LLM changed only what the developer authorised. We present CAPRI, a contract-aware repair workflow in which Isabelle checks the proof and an independent checker enforces a machine-readable edit contract. Prompts, proposals, candidate repositories, diagnostics, verdicts, and hashes are retained for audit. We evaluate five workflows on twelve failed proofs from four developments, with three replicates per task and condition, giving 180 runs and 138 valid repairs. Of 144 terminal candidates accepted by Isabelle, six had modified protected text; all arose in iterative workflows that could edit a complete theory. A proof-body-only interface produced 29/36 valid repairs and no contract violations, compared with 31/36 for the corresponding full-theory workflow. One-shot repair produced 22/36, while a later prospectively frozen iterative workflow produced 32/36; these figures compare complete workflows rather than individual mechanisms. A separate post hoc OpenRouter campaign found no improvement in the designated Luna comparisons. A Sol configuration with matched demonstrations produced 33/36 repairs, compared with 29/36 in the frozen OpenAI Responses condition, but the difference was not statistically significant in a one-sided exact McNemar test ($p=0.0625$).

</details>


### [116] [Can Formal Specifications Be Synthesized from Tests Alone?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13240)
*Tianhai Liu,Maximilian Müller,Tobias Hey,Vitus Lüntzel,Muhammad Minhas,Anne Koziolek,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向黑盒场景的规格推断方法：仅利用测试代码与动态执行轨迹，由大语言模型生成候选 Java Modeling Language（JML）规格，并通过有界模型检查反馈迭代修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工编写形式化规格成本高。现有基于大语言模型的自动推断通常需要访问源代码实现细节，可能带来知识产权暴露风险及部署成本。该工作旨在降低对实现可见性的依赖，使规格推断更适用于实际软件开发环境。

Method: 系统向大语言模型提供程序接口、选定测试输入及其对应输出或状态变化，而不提供实现内部信息。模型据此生成候选规格；随后在本地使用有界模型检查验证候选规格，并将检查器诊断与验证反馈用于下一轮生成和细化。

Result: 在 SpecGenBench 基准上的初步实验表明，测试及动态轨迹能够引导模型推断出具有实际语义的 JML 规格。实验同时发现，模型输出与验证工具的兼容性，以及诊断反馈的质量和可用性，是影响迭代优化可靠性的关键因素。

Conclusion: 测试驱动、黑盒式的大语言模型规格推断具有可行性，能够在隐藏实现细节的条件下辅助生成形式化规格。但要获得稳定可靠的结果，仍需改进规格语法与检查器之间的兼容机制，并增强模型对验证诊断信息的利用能力。

Abstract: Formal specifications offer strong guarantees, but remain costly to write manually. Recent LLM-based approaches automate this by inferring specifications from source code, yet their reliance on white-box access poses barriers to industrial adoption due to intellectual property risks and deployment costs. Our approach uses LLMs to infer candidate specifications solely from test code and dynamic execution traces: the LLM observes only the program interface, selected inputs, and corresponding outputs or state changes, while the implementation internals remain hidden. Candidate specifications are validated locally using bounded model checking, with feedback guiding iterative refinement. Initial results on the SpecGenBench benchmark suggest that tests can guide LLMs towards meaningful Java Modeling Language specifications, while also highlighting checker compatibility and diagnostic feedback as key challenges for reliable refinement.

</details>


### [117] [Refine After Generation: Toward Correct and Concise Patches in LLM-based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13292)
*Wenqiang Luo,Jacky Keung,Xiaoyu Shi,Yicheng Sun,Boyang Yang,Zhou Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出，基于大语言模型的自动程序修复虽然能够解决真实仓库级问题，但生成补丁普遍过于冗长、复杂，影响可审查性。作者提出后生成补丁精炼方法RECAP，在基本保持甚至提升修复成功率的同时显著减少代码改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动程序修复主要以测试是否通过作为评价标准，对补丁规模、复杂度和可维护性关注不足。研究发现，LLM生成的成功补丁通常明显大于开发者补丁，而简单的提示词或输出格式约束难以有效降低冗余，因此需要将补丁最小化作为独立的后处理阶段。

Method: 作者在SWE-bench Verified上分析28种先进方法，比较其与开发者补丁在总改动量、净改动量和圈复杂度方面的差异，并研究迭代修正、宽上下文等设计因素对补丁冗长性的影响。随后提出可插拔的后生成精炼器RECAP，使用来自多个来源的补丁对数据，通过监督微调和直接偏好优化，并结合蒸馏推理轨迹进行训练。

Result: LLM方法生成的成功补丁中位数总改动量增加121.78%，净改动量增加80.91%，圈复杂度提高43.99%。在四个宿主系统上，提示优化、提交解缠和最小化基线虽然能缩小补丁，却损失49至217个已解决实例。RECAP将相对开发者补丁的平均总改动量从增加242.14%降至增加4.24%，净改动量从增加348.24%降至减少39.75%，同时保持或最多增加42个修复成功实例。

Conclusion: 补丁最小化不能简单等同于语法压缩；将生成与精炼解耦能够在不牺牲正确性的情况下显著提升补丁的简洁性和可审查性。RECAP为现有自动程序修复框架提供了一种轻量、通用且实用的增强方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automatic program repair (APR) to the point where agentic systems routinely resolve real-world, repository-level issues. Yet the generated patch has received little scrutiny beyond whether it passes tests.
  In this paper, we identify patch verbosity as a major yet overlooked concern in LLM-based APR. Characterizing 28 state-of-the-art approaches on SWE-bench Verified, we find that even successful patches are consistently larger and more complex than developer patches, with the median approach producing 121.78% more total changes, 80.91% more net changes, and 43.99% higher cyclomatic complexity. We further show that this verbosity is rooted in capability-oriented design choices such as iterative refinement and broad context, and can hardly be reduced by surface-level controls such as output format or minimality prompts. Motivated by these findings, we formulate post-generation patch refinement and propose RECAP, a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter that attaches to existing repair frameworks after generation. RECAP's refiner is trained via supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization with distilled reasoning traces, on a dataset of patch pairs we construct from multiple sources. Across four host systems, prompting, commit-untangling, and minimality-aware baselines reduce patch size only by sacrificing 49 to 217 resolved instances. In contrast, RECAP achieves a substantially better size-correctness tradeoff, cutting average total changes from +242.14% to +4.24% and net changes from +348.24% to -39.75% relative to developer patches while preserving or improving resolution by up to 42 instances. Our results indicate that minimality cannot be simply reduced to syntactic compression, and that decoupling minimization from generation offers a practical path to more reviewable repairs.

</details>


### [118] [Integration-First Structural Coverage for Embedded Software:Trace-Based Evidence, Hybrid Runtime Analysis, and Cross-Variant Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13322)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz,Michael Wittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出面向嵌入式软件的集成优先覆盖率方案：以设备上的集成与系统测试作为主要度量来源，结合嵌入式追踪、混合运行时分析（hRA）和跨构建证据汇聚，在接近发布配置的-O3优化二进制上实现可审查的分支、条件与MC/DC覆盖测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统结构覆盖率多在单元测试阶段采集，因为插桩和可观测性成本低；但这与真实设备上的集成及系统行为存在代表性差距。直接在目标设备上做经典插桩会扰动时序、内存占用和并发行为，而仅依赖追踪重建在高优化编译下又难以可靠恢复判定与条件覆盖，因此需要兼顾真实性、低扰动性与证据可信度的方法。

Method: 过程上，采用“集成优先”策略：先以集成和系统测试建立覆盖基线，再通过显式闭环处理剩余缺口，将完整性定义为“已覆盖或已合理说明”。技术上，以嵌入式追踪作为观测通道，并引入hRA，通过最小化且保持语义不变的可观测性支架，使优化后二进制的判定与条件边界仍可在追踪流中区分；覆盖状态和计数均放在目标设备之外。随后利用Hyper Coverage汇聚不同测试层级、测试运行、软件变体和构建配置的覆盖证据。

Result: 该方案将目标代码到源代码的映射由依赖启发式的重建转化为可复核证据；在-O3等发布级优化构建中，可实际开展分支、条件和MC/DC覆盖度量。Hyper Coverage还能识别在所有相关变体中均未被测试到的源代码行，并统一展示多来源覆盖证据。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将覆盖率评估从低成本但代表性有限的单元级插桩，转向真实设备上的集成级证据采集，同时通过hRA降低观测扰动并提升优化代码覆盖映射的可靠性。该方法强调覆盖闭环与覆盖豁免说明，有助于形成更接近实际交付配置的软件测试完整性论证。

Abstract: Structural coverage is widely used as evidence that testing is complete, yet in embedded projects it is predominantly collected at unit level, simply because that is where instrumentation and observability are inexpensive. This produces a mismatch. The most representative completeness signal would come from integration and system tests executed on the device under test, but classical instrumentation perturbs timing, memory footprint and concurrency behaviour, while purely trace-reconstructed coverage loses reliability for decisions and conditions as soon as the compiler optimizes aggressively. We address this mismatch from both ends. On the process side we describe an integrationfirst coverage strategy that treats integration and system tests as the baseline measurement and drives the residual gaps through an explicit closure loop, so that completeness is established as covered or justified rather than as covered alone. On the technical side we use embedded trace as the observation path and add hybrid runtime analysis (hRA): a minimal, semantics-preserving observability scaffolding that keeps decision and condition boundaries distinguishable in the trace stream of an optimized (-O3) build, while all coverage state and counting remain off-target. This converts object-to-source mapping from a heuristic reconstruction into reviewable evidence and makes branch, condition and MC/DC measurement practical on release-like binaries. Finally we describe Hyper Coverage, a consolidation layer that merges evidence across test levels, test runs, variants and build configurations, and that exposes source lines which remain untested in every relevant variant.

</details>


### [119] [Does Fixing Break Security? An Empirical Study of Security Degradation in Iterative LLM-Driven Infrastructure-as-Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13404)
*Benjamin Agyekum,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文首次基于逐轮原始检查结果，而非单调递增的累计最优指标，量化了大语言模型迭代修复基础设施即代码时的安全回归。严格定义下，约3.3%的场景发生真实回归；多数表面回归源于多资源重构导致的测量现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代式IaC修复通常依赖验证器反馈持续修复，但累计最优指标会掩盖某一轮修复在解决原有问题的同时引入新安全失败的风险。研究旨在明确安全回归的发生频率、根因、动态演化及适宜的停止轮次。

Method: 作者分析IaC-Eval中的5,968条场景时间线，覆盖15种配置、最多5轮修复，并对具有相邻两轮Checkov数据的4,440次迭代转换进行比较。研究跟踪30个CIS检查标识，依据代码差异归类根因；同时采用标准检测模式与仅计入排他性检查失败的严格检测模式，以区分真实回归和多资源变动引起的表观回归。

Result: 标准模式下，13.8%的场景和24.8%的迭代转换至少出现一次回归；严格模式下分别降至3.3%和5.2%，说明大多数表面回归属于多资源测量伪象。资源重构占根因的79.0%。发生回归的转换具有2.6倍更高的代码变动量，效应量Cohen's d为0.90；严格模式下检查波动性高4.9倍，d为1.49。标准模式回归中36.6%会在平均1.2轮后自行修正，第三轮是最优停止点。

Conclusion: 迭代式大语言模型IaC修复确实可能损害先前通过的安全检查，但可保守认定的真实场景级回归率约为3.3%。系统应采用安全感知的反馈循环，针对大规模资源重构和高代码变动设置额外审查，并将修复预算优先限制在约三轮，以降低后续迭代的风险与收益失衡。

Abstract: Background: Iterative feedback loops are the dominant paradigm for improving LLM-generated Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC): validators such as Checkov and terraform validate feed error signals back for successive repair attempts. Prior work reports cumulative-best metrics, which are non-decreasing by construction, so the raw per-iteration security trajectory has never been examined for IaC. Aims: We study security regression (a previously-passing CIS Benchmark check that fails after a repair iteration) to determine whether and how often iterative LLM repair degrades security while fixing other issues. Method: We analyze 5,968 scenario timelines from the IaC-Eval benchmark, each one scenario run through one configuration for up to 5 repair iterations. The 15 configurations (six model-specific RAG, nine model-aggregated non-RAG, three temperatures each) yield 4,440 iteration transitions with Checkov data on both sides. We track 30 individual CIS check IDs and classify root causes from code diffs, under two detection modes: standard (inclusive) and strict (exclusive check failures only). Results: Under standard detection, 13.8% of scenarios (24.8% of transitions) exhibit at least one regression. Under strict detection the rate falls to 3.3% of scenarios (5.2% of transitions), indicating most apparent regressions are multi-resource measurement artifacts. Resource restructuring (79.0%) is the dominant root cause. Regression transitions show 2.6x more code churn (Cohen's d=0.90) and 4.9x higher strict-mode check volatility (d=1.49). Of standard-mode regressions, 36.6% self-correct within an average of 1.2 iterations; iteration 3 is the optimal stopping point. Conclusions: Iterative IaC repair does introduce security regressions, but the conservative, defensible rate is about 3.3% of scenarios. Our findings motivate security-aware feedback-loop design and actionable iteration-budget guidance.

</details>


### [120] [LLM-Assisted Dynamic Threat Analysis for Attacker-Reachable Software Weaknesses in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13450)
*Md Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文评估大语言模型辅助对Autoware进行动态安全分析的可行性。结论是：静态候选点生成并非主要瓶颈，真正限制端到端验证的是将生成的测试桩、依赖和构建配置正确接入真实大型软件栈；在给定预算内未动态确认任何候选缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶软件包含大量影响控制决策的安全关键代码。静态分析能够发现潜在风险位置，但要证明其可实际触发，需要可编译、可执行并能进行模糊测试的测试工件。人工构造这类工件成本高，论文因此考察LLM能否自动生成并修复它们。

Method: 作者对185个软件包进行编译器精确的静态分析，提取1,375条决策规则、2,274项校验和482条输入至安全输出的数据流，并从中采样740个可达位置。实验比较两种本地开源权重LLM、一个不提供静态上下文的消融设置及朴素模板基线，共生成3,700套测试工件。工件在真实构建环境中结合Sanitizer编译，通过编译器反馈循环修复；可执行工件再进入模糊测试。

Result: 首次编译失败中，80%源于依赖连接、构建接口和环境集成问题，而非测试逻辑错误。推理型模型的测试桩首次编译成功率为64%，代码专用模型仅为6%。推理型模型虽然可通过大量桩替代实现对象级完全可编译，但不足一半测试桩能够实际进入模糊测试。共观察到37次崩溃，均发生在替代桩代码中，而非Autoware原始代码；在实验预算内没有动态确认任何静态候选缺陷。

Conclusion: LLM在生成候选测试代码方面具有一定能力，但对完整自动驾驶软件栈而言，可靠动态分析的关键挑战是构建与依赖集成。未来系统应优先改进真实依赖解析、接口语义恢复、最小可执行环境构建和对桩代码伪阳性的隔离，而不应仅提升候选生成或模糊测试策略。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on large safety-critical software stacks, where weaknesses reachable from adversarial inputs may affect steering, braking, or other control decisions. Static analysis can identify candidate sites, but dynamically confirming exploitability requires executable test artifacts that are difficult to construct manually. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this process for Autoware, an open-source autonomous-driving stack. We perform compiler-precise static analysis across 185 packages, identifying 1,375 decision rules, 2,274 validation checks, and 482 input-to-safety-output flows, from which we derive a weakness taxonomy and sample 740 reachable sites. Two local open-weight LLMs, a no-static-context ablation, and a naive-template baseline generate 3,700 artifact sets, which are compiled against the real build under sanitizers, repaired through compiler-in-the-loop feedback, and fuzzed when executable. The main result is a build-integration failure taxonomy showing that 80% of first-shot compilation failures arise from dependency wiring rather than program logic. The reasoning model compiled 64% of harnesses on the first attempt, compared with 6% for the code-specialized model. Repair achieved full object-compileability for the reasoning model only through extensive stubbing; fewer than half of its harnesses reached the fuzzer, and all 37 observed crashes originated in stubbed code rather than Autoware. No candidate weakness was dynamically confirmed within budget. These results show that build integration, not candidate generation or fuzzing, is the primary barrier to reliable LLM-assisted dynamic analysis of full autonomous-vehicle software stacks.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [A Rig of Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12409)
*Emma Tye*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出以可交换半环刻画有限代数数据类型，并将半环等式对应为数据类型间的同构；同时使用部分同构描述类型嵌入更大表示类型的情形，如位填充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶函数式语言的编译器通常限制开发者对数据运行时二进制表示的控制。然而，程序层面的数据建模与面向效率、兼容既有格式等需求的底层布局可能不同，因此需要一种既正确又高效且易用的布局描述与转换机制。

Method: 作者将有限代数数据类型建模为可交换半环：类型的组合关系由半环运算表达，满足的半环等式则由数据之间的可逆同构实现。对于无法完全可逆的表示扩展，采用部分同构建模，即只在满足特定约束的值域内实现嵌入与恢复。

Result: 该框架能够统一表达有限数据类型的等价重构，以及程序数据模型到目标二进制布局之间的结构化转换。它还可处理向更大类型嵌入的场景，例如为满足对齐或存储需求而加入未承载语义信息的位。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是以半环代数和同构为基础，为有限代数数据类型的布局变换提供形式化模型；部分同构进一步扩展了模型对填充等非完全双射表示转换的适用性。摘要未提供具体实现、性能评估或人体工程学验证结果。

Abstract: In high-level functional languages, the compiler often gives users little control over the runtime representation of data types. Yet how we model data structures at the program level can be different to how we want to represent them at the binary level, for efficiency or legacy reasons. Hence being able to describe data layouts and their transformations for data types is a useful and necessary part of programming, but difficult to do correctly, efficiently and ergonomically. We present a model of finite algebraic data types as a commutative rig (a ring without additive inverses), where the rig-equalities are modelled by isomorphisms between data. Using this approach, we can also model embedding a data type into a larger type (e.g. bit-padding) as a partial isomorphism.

</details>


### [122] [Stream-based Online and Offline Monitoring under Measurement Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13211)
*Bernd Finkbeiner,Martin Fränzle,Florian Kohn,Paul Kröger*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出RLola，一种面向含噪传感器数据的鲁棒流式运行时验证语言。其通过松弛变量显式符号化测量误差，支持在线实时监控与离线全轨迹分析，并给出可保证常数内存在线执行的语言片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流式监控通常将传感器输入视为精确物理测量，但实际传感器存在噪声和误差，且这些误差会在监控中的计算与聚合过程中放大。直接采用区间算术又可能产生别名问题，导致验证结论过度保守或不精确，因此需要能够可靠处理不确定测量值的规范与监控机制。

Method: RLola在Lola语言基础上引入松弛变量，以符号方式表示测量噪声并规避区间算术的别名效应。论文设计两类算法：在线算法持续处理实时输入并反馈断言状态；离线算法基于可满足性模理论分析完整轨迹，判断是否存在满足所有时刻断言的假设真实轨迹。针对一般RLola规范可能需要无界内存的问题，作者进一步识别出一个可自动编译为常数内存在线监控器的丰富子语言。算法被实现于现有RTLola框架。

Result: 在线监控器能够面向实时系统提供当前断言状态，但可能遗漏只有结合完整轨迹才能识别的问题；离线算法通过全局约束求解可发现这类问题。对于所定义的受限但表达力较强的RLola片段，在线监控具有常数内存保证。实现评估以综合示例考察了方法的精度与运行时间。摘要未给出具体的定量实验指标。

Conclusion: RLola将传感器不确定性纳入流式运行时验证的语义与算法设计中，在避免区间别名问题的同时，兼顾实时反馈和离线精确分析。其常数内存片段增强了实际部署可行性，而SMT离线分析为在线监控提供了更强的完备性补充。

Abstract: Stream-based monitoring is a runtime verification approach for cyber-physical systems that translates streams of input data, such as sensor readings, into streams of aggregate statistics and verdicts about the safety of the system. It is usually assumed that the values on the input streams represent fully accurate measurements of the physical world. In reality, however, physical sensors are prone to measurement noise and errors. These errors are further amplified by the processing and aggregation steps within the monitor. This paper introduces RLola, a robust extension of the stream-based specification language Lola. RLola incorporates the concept of slack variables, which symbolically represent measurement noise while avoiding the aliasing problem of interval arithmetic.
  We present algorithms for both online and offline monitoring of RLola specifications. Since monitoring RLola specifications may require unbounded memory in general, we identify a rich fragment of RLola that can be automatically translated into monitors with guaranteed constant memory usage for online monitoring. An online RLola monitor observes a live system and provides real-time feedback on the current status of specified assertions. A satisfiability-modulo-theories-based offline algorithm analyzes complete system traces and determines whether a hypothetical ground-truth trace exists that satisfies all assertions at all time points. The offline algorithm can therefore detect violations that the online algorithm may miss. We implement these algorithms in the existing RTLola framework and evaluate their precision and running time based on a comprehensive example.

</details>


### [123] [Multiobjective Preexpectation Reasoning for Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13268)
*Lena Verscht,Hannah Mertens,Kevin Batz,Sebastian Junges,Benjamin Lucien Kaminski,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 论文提出面向含非确定性的概率程序的多目标策略综合框架，在程序语义层面刻画帕累托最优可达结果，并支持无限状态空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率程序验证常聚焦单一期望目标，而规划问题往往需同时权衡多个目标。作者旨在为多目标优化提供可演绎、可证明正确的程序级策略构造方法。

Method: 定义多目标前期望变换器，将后期望元组映射为可同时实现的值集合；该集合采用凸Hoare幂域表示。该变换器保守扩展最弱前期望语义，并提升标准循环推理规则。进一步给出策略综合规则，将见证策略构造为对非概率确定化策略进行随机选择的混合确定化策略；最后依据操作型MDP语义证明其正确性。

Result: 所提变换器与策略综合规则在操作语义下成立，且不依赖有限状态空间假设。因此，该方法可视为针对无限MDP多目标优化的符号化、程序级分析技术。论文还通过多个案例展示了框架的适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作将多目标帕累托优化、概率程序语义和策略综合统一到凸集合值前期望框架中，既保留传统最弱前期望推理能力，又能生成满足多目标权衡要求的随机化策略。

Abstract: Probabilistic programs with nondeterminism model planning problems in which a strategy resolves the nondeterminism to optimize an expected outcome. We study the multiobjective setting, optimizing several outcomes at once along a Pareto front, and provide a deductive, program-level account of strategy synthesis. Its core is a multiobjective preexpectation transformer mapping a tuple of postexpectations to the set of simultaneously achievable values, an element of the convex Hoare powerdomain. It conservatively extends weakest preexpectations and lifts standard loop rules. We develop rules to synthesize witnessing strategies as mixed determinizations that randomize over non-probabilistic determinizations. We prove the transformer and synthesis rules sound against an operational MDP semantics, without requiring a finite state space: our approach can be seen as a symbolic approach - at program level - for multiobjective optimization over infinite MDPs. We demonstrate our machinery using various case studies.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Synchronous Observers Revisited for Runtime Verification of Lustre Using STL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12693)
*Logan Kenwright,Partha Roop,Sobhan Chatterjee,Nathan Allen*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出将同步信号时序逻辑（SSTL）自动编译为Lustre同步观察器的方法，使同一规格既可用于在线运行时监控，也可借助Kind2进行静态验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STL常用于网络物理系统的运行时验证，而同步语言通常以可组合的观察器表达安全属性。现有STL到同步观察器的工作对嵌套有界公式支持有限，且较少统一运行时与静态验证流程。

Method: 作者采用模块化编译，将SSTL公式映射为Lustre数据流模块。该方法支持任意层次的有界时序算子嵌套，并允许最外层使用全局无界算子以适配在线监控；同时开发交互式可视化工具，在可编辑轨迹上展示公式的三值判定结果。

Result: 生成的Lustre观察器可作为可执行监控规格进行运行时验证，并可直接与Kind2模型检查器结合完成静态验证。方法在弹簧—质量系统和跟车巡航控制器两个文献案例上进行了评估。摘要未给出具体性能数据或定量比较结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了SSTL、Lustre观察器、运行时监控和静态模型检查之间的统一编译链路；其主要贡献是通过模块化设计提升对复杂嵌套有界公式的表达能力，并以无界外层算子支持持续在线判定。

Abstract: Signal Temporal Logic (STL) is a popular formalism for the temporal safety properties of cyber-physical systems, most often used for runtime verification. In the synchronous family of languages, safety properties are instead expressed as synchronous observers, modules composed with a program for static verification, which are also runnable specifications suitable for runtime verification, though this use is rarely explored. We present a technique for compiling the synchronous fragment of STL (SSTL) into synchronous observers in the dataflow language Lustre. Unlike previous work, we allow arbitrary nesting of bounded SSTL properties via modular compilation, and admit a globally unbounded outer operator for online monitoring; the resulting observers serve both runtime verification and, as a by-product, static verification with the Kind2 model checker. We further contribute an interactive visualiser that renders a property's three-valued verdict over an editable trace, and evaluate on two case studies from the literature: a spring-mass system and a car-following cruise controller.

</details>


### [125] [Computing Fixed Points using Dependency Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13020)
*Giorgio Bacci,Giovanni Bacci,Kim G. Larsen,Daniele Toller*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文提出了针对带底元的诺特偏序集上方程组求解的全局与局部算法。其核心是通过依赖预言机按需发现变量依赖，仅探索确定目标变量取值所必需的方程部分，并在保证正确性的前提下支持精度与性能之间的可控权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多验证任务可归约为偏序域上的方程组求解，但传统全局求解往往遍历完整系统，局部算法则可能缺乏通用且可靠的终止判据。论文旨在构建一种适用范围广、实现简洁、能够针对指定变量进行按需计算的统一求解框架。

Method: 方法同时设计全局和局部求解算法。局部算法以目标变量为起点，利用依赖预言机动态识别影响该变量值的其他变量，并据此限制状态空间或方程空间的探索范围。预言机还为局部不动点迭代提供可靠的终止条件。该框架允许对预言机进行定制、组合及保守近似：近似依赖关系可能扩大探索范围，但不破坏求解结果的正确性。

Result: 作者将原型实现与已有文献中的算法进行比较。实验表明，该方案具有竞争力，并且在不少场景中优于专用求解方法；同时，它保持了较低的实现复杂度，并可适应多个应用领域。摘要未给出具体基准、运行时间、内存占用或统计显著性等定量数据。

Conclusion: 依赖预言机将“求哪些变量、何时停止局部迭代”抽象为可替换组件，使得偏序方程组的局部求解兼具正确性、模块化和适应性。该工作的重要价值在于提供统一的通用框架，而非仅针对某一种验证问题设计专用算法。

Abstract: We present global and local algorithms for solving systems of equations over Noetherian posets with a bottom element, a general setting underlying many verification problems. Our algorithms compute the solution of a selected variable by restricting exploration to those parts of the system required to determine its value. We achieve this by computing variable dependencies by means of dependency oracles. Oracles guide the exploration of the system and provide sound termination criteria for local fixed-point computation. A key advantage of our approach is its flexibility: oracles can be customized, composed, or over-approximated, offering a principled way to trade precision for performance without compromising correctness. We evaluate our solution against existing algorithms from the literature and show that our prototype implementation is competitive and often outperforms specialized solutions, while remaining simple and adaptable across diverse application domains.

</details>


### [126] [Completeness and incompleteness of basic matching logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13306)
*Xiaohong Chen,Grigore Rosu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文系统刻画了基本匹配逻辑的全局完备性边界：任意单排序、有限元签名且无不动点时，全局语义蕴含与可证性等价；加入不动点后有效公理化失效；扩展到三排序时全局完备性可失败。相应结论还推广为一类模态语言在无名词时完备、加入名词后在特定良基演算类别中不可全局完备的二分现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本匹配逻辑缺少用于统一表达全定义性的联结词，其无限假设集下的全局完备性并不直接由常规局部完备性推出。论文旨在解决单排序情形的完备性猜想，并精确识别排序数、不动点和名词等语言特征对可证明性及有效公理化的影响。

Method: 作者将任意假设集Γ局部化为理论Δ_Γ，并证明Γ的全局语义蕴含、Δ_Γ下的局部语义蕴含与Γ上的推导关系三者等价。关键语义步骤采用双覆盖构造。负面结果则通过编码不动点带来的计算不可判定性、分析多排序间的排序流动，并针对混合逻辑中遵守局部化的良基演算构造普遍障碍。

Result: 对任意单排序有限元签名、无不动点的基本匹配逻辑，得到Γ⊨φ当且仅当Γ⊢φ，即使Γ可为无限集；由此推出带定义性扩展是保守的。若存在一个一元和两个二元符号且无常量，不动点语言的有效性并非递归可枚举，因此不存在证明关系递归可枚举且即便弱完备的可靠演算。三排序时，某些可满足Γ已导致全局完备性失败。对于相应的任意元模态语言，无名词时全局完备；加入名词后，所述良基局部化演算类均无法全局完备。

Conclusion: 全局完备性在该框架中并非普遍性质，而是受语言结构严格制约：单排序、无不动点是可获得全局完备性的关键条件；不动点破坏有效可公理化性，多排序和名词则分别造成更强的全局完备性障碍。论文不仅证明单排序猜想，也给出了其在排序数量与语言扩展维度上的尖锐边界。

Abstract: Basic matching logic is matching logic without definedness. Symbols are interpreted as set-valued operations, element variables denote singletons and are bound by $\exists$, and no connective uniformly internalizes totality. For basic matching logic without fixpoints over an arbitrary one-sorted finitary signature, we prove \emph{global completeness} ($Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$, for arbitrary, possibly infinite $Γ$) and, as a corollary, conservativity of the definedness extension. The proof localizes $Γ$ to a theory $Δ_Γ$ and reduces semantic consequence and derivability to the same local relation: $Γ\vDash\varphi$ iff $Δ_Γ\vDash_\text{loc}\varphi$ iff $Γ\vdash\varphi$. A double-cover construction establishes the semantic equivalence. Least fixpoints destroy effective axiomatizability. Over a signature with one unary and two binary symbols and no constants, validity is not recursively enumerable; hence no sound calculus with a recursively enumerable proof relation is even weakly complete, already for the empty theory and without definedness. The positive result is also sharp in the number of sorts. Global completeness fails with three sorts for a satisfiable $Γ$. Thus the completeness conjecture holds for one sort and fails in general. The negative results arise from sort flow, fixpoint effectivity, and, for hybrid logic, an obstruction to every well-founded calculus whose leaves are hypotheses or valid patterns and whose rules respect localization. This yields a matching-logic-independent dichotomy: the language with state variables bound by $\exists$ and $\forall$ over modalities of arbitrary arity is globally complete without nominals, while no calculus in that well-founded class is globally complete once nominals are added.

</details>


### [127] [A Dense Weisfeiler-Leman Algorithm for Deciding Bounded-Cliquewidth Homomorphism Indistinguishability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13382)
*Radu Curticapean,Daniel Neuen,Amir Nikabadi,Tim Seppelt,Ben Young*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文研究“相对于图类的同态不可区分性”的可判定性，首次为稠密图类给出一般性算法：提出稠密 Weisfeiler-Leman 算法以判定对团宽至多 k 的图类的同态不可区分性，并将结果推广至有界团宽的 CMSO_1 可定义图类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态计数刻画统一了多种图同构松弛，但当测试图类无限时，其不可区分性未必可判定；平面图类上甚至不可判定。既有可判定性结果主要限于稀疏图类，因此需要建立适用于由团宽刻画的稠密图类的判定理论与复杂度界。

Method: 作者定义稠密 Weisfeiler-Leman 算法。不同于标准 WL 对顶点 k 元组进行着色，该算法对“顶点子集的 k 元组”进行着色，以捕获团宽至多 k 图类上的同态信息。在此基础上，利用 CMSO_1 逻辑定义性与有界团宽结构，证明适用于任意 CMSO_1 可定义且团宽有界图类的元定理；并针对有界线性团宽图类改进空间复杂度分析。

Result: 稠密 WL 算法可判定相对于团宽至多 k 图类的同态不可区分性，该关系此前未知是否可判定。进一步地，对每个 CMSO_1 可定义的有界团宽图类，该问题可在随机化指数时间内判定；若图类具有有界线性团宽，则可在 PSPACE 内判定。作者还构造了一个有界线性团宽图类，使该问题 PSPACE 完全，证明该上界紧致。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为稠密图类上的同态不可区分性提供通用可判定算法和复杂度刻画，将基于同态计数的图同构松弛理论从传统稀疏结构扩展到团宽框架，并揭示了线性团宽情形下 PSPACE 复杂度的本质界限。

Abstract: Two graphs $G$ and $H$ are homomorphism indistinguishable over a graph class $\mathcal{F}$ if they admit the same number of homomorphisms from every graph in $\mathcal{F}$. A wide range of relaxations of graph isomorphism arise this way: isomorphism itself over the class of all graphs [Lovász, Acta Math. Hung. 1967], equivalence under the $k$-dimensional Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm over the graphs of treewidth $\leq k$ [Dvořák, J. Graph Theory 2010], and quantum isomorphism over planar graphs [Mančinska-Roberson, FOCS 2020]. Since the class $\mathcal{F}$ is typically infinite, it is not clear a priori whether homomorphism indistinguishability over $\mathcal{F}$ is decidable; for planar graphs it is undecidable. Every class for which decidability was previously known is sparse.
  We give the first decidability results for dense graph classes: We introduce the dense Weisfeiler-Leman algorithm that decides homomorphism indistinguishability over the class of graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, the dense counterpart of treewidth. This relation was not previously known to be decidable. The algorithm colors $k$-tuples of vertex subsets rather than $k$-tuples of vertices.
  Beyond the class of all graphs of cliquewidth $\leq k$, we prove a general meta-theorem: homomorphism indistinguishability over every $\mathsf{CMSO}_1$-definable graph class of bounded cliquewidth is decidable, in randomized exponential time. For classes of bounded linear cliquewidth the bound improves to $\mathsf{PSPACE}$, and we show this is tight by exhibiting such a class for which the problem is $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete. These are the first general algorithms for homomorphism indistinguishability over dense graph classes.

</details>


### [128] [Runtime Monitoring of Distributed Cyber-Physical Systems Without a Global Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13486)
*Charles Koll,Houssam Abbas*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 论文针对多智能体分布式信息物理系统在本地时钟存在漂移、缺乏确定全局时间时，如何在线监测连续时间时序逻辑性质的问题，提出了首个理论刻画与算法，并给出可扩展的健全近似实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制与系统设计通常以全局时间理解系统行为，但分布式系统中的各智能体仅有彼此漂移的本地时钟。现有监测方法多面向离散时间，或要求预先将公式的时间约束显式映射到各本地时钟，因此难以直接用于具有连续动态的分布式信息物理系统。

Method: 作者面向仍包含全部时序算子的一个信号时序逻辑片段，扩展了传统满足信号的定义，使其适用于部分同步时钟模型；同时分析多维部分同步时间的几何结构。据此设计算法，计算在所有与本地观测及时钟漂移一致的解释下，可能满足公式的全局时刻集合，并推导其最坏情况复杂度。随后实现该算法的健全近似版本以支持实际运行。

Result: 算法输出的是可能使规格成立的全部全局时刻，而非依赖单一假定的全局时间线。理论上，工作给出了相应监测问题的刻画及算法复杂度；实验上，健全近似实现展示了有效监测能力，并可处理最多50个智能体的场景。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了连续时间分布式信息物理系统在时钟漂移条件下进行时序逻辑监测的理论与算法空白。其核心贡献是将全局时间不确定性显式纳入满足语义和监测计算，为分布式混合控制系统的调试与验证提供了可操作的方法。

Abstract: We give the first theoretical characterization, and the first algorithm, for continuous monitoring of a distributed Cyber-Physical System (CPS) against a dense-time temporal logic specification. A distributed CPS is composed of multiple agents, each with a local clock; these clocks drift from each other, so there is no well-defined global time. When monitoring such a system's output signal against a temporal logic specification, it is not evident how to interpret the temporal constraints of the formula, and what satisfaction means. Yet CPS designers, like control engineers, typically think of their system's operation in terms of global time. Most existing techniques for monitoring distributed systems work with discrete-time specifications not suitable for CPS, and/or require an explicit mapping of temporal constraints to local clocks. We introduce an algorithm that addresses the above challenges for a fragment of Signal Temporal Logic (STL) that still includes all temporal operators. It relies on a novel extension of satisfaction signals to this partially synchronous setting (where clocks drift), and an analysis of the geometry of multi-dimensional partially synchronous time. The algorithm returns the set of all possible global moments that can satisfy the specification. Knowledge of these possible global moments is important for debugging distributed hybrid control systems such as fleets of drones and electrical grids. We derive the worst-case complexity of the algorithm, and implement a sound approximation of it that experimentally illustrates effective monitoring, even in scenarios of up to 50 agents.

</details>
